On the equivalence of first and second order formulations of the Einstein-Hilbert theory
F. T. Brandt, J. Frenkel, S. Martins-Filho, D. G. C. McKeon

Abstract
We derive a general relation between the background effective actions, which directly proves that the two formulations of the Einstein-Hilbert theory with background fields are equivalent at the quantum level. This basic result has been substantiated in a general background gauge, by explicit calculations at one-loop order of the corresponding counterterm Lagrangians.



I
Introduction

The first order formulation of gauge field theories involves Lagrangians that are linear in time derivatives, unlike the second order formulation, which features quadratic time derivative terms. In addition to the original fields, the first order approach introduces independent fields—such as the affine connection in gravity—which can lead to significant simplifications in the structure of the interaction vertices. For instance, the first order formulations of the Yang–Mills and Einstein–Hilbert (EH) theories contain only a simple cubic interaction, whereas the second order versions typically involve a large number of more complex terms. This structural improvement makes the first order approach more appropriate for quantum calculations
l. Buchbinder and l. Shapiro 1983
;
McKeon 1994
;
Kalmykov and Pronin 1995
;
Martellini and Zeni 1997
;
Brandt and McKeon 2015
;
Brandt and McKeon 2016
;
Brandt et al. 2020a
;
Brandt et al. 2020b
.

Such formulations and their equivalence have been investigated from many points of view, both without
Okubo and Tosa 1979
;
Taubes 1980
;
Castellani et al. 1982
;
Tseytlin 1982
;
Accardi et al. 1997
;
Kiriushcheva et al. 2006
;
Andrasi and Taylor 2009
;
McKeon 2010
;
Dadhich and Pons 2012
;
López et al. 2014
;
Frenkel and Taylor 2017
;
Benisty and Guendelman 2018
;
Tenkanen 2020
;
Brandt et al. 2020a
;
Brandt et al. 2020b
;
Lavrov 2021
;
Gallagher et al. 2022
;
Rigouzzo and Zell 2023
and with background field quantization
Buchbinder and Shapiro 1985
;
Alvarez et al. 2021
;
Brandt et al. 2019
;
Brandt et al. 2024a
;
Brandt et al. 2024b
. In the background field method
Abbott 1981
;
Weinberg 2000
, gauge invariance is preserved at each stage by splitting the fields into classical background and quantum parts. It has been observed that, when the background affine connection is on-shell, the effective actions in the first and second order formulations coincide. While earlier works have demonstrated this equivalence in a variety of ways, our goal here is to present a more direct and transparent derivation, confirmed by explicit one-loop calculations in a general background gauge.

The distinction between first- and second-order formulations of gravity is not merely a matter of formalism. It reflects different choices of fundamental variables, and potentially different paths to quantization.
Establishing their quantum equivalence is also essential for confirming the internal consistency of more general models, such as the Einstein–Cartan theory
Hehl et al. 1976
;
Shapiro 2002
;
Hehl 2023
or supergravity
Van Nieuwenhuizen 1981
, and of the background field approach.

In Sec. II, we derive a relation between the generating functionals of connected Green’s functions in the first and second order formulations of the EH theory. From this, we obtain a basic identity between the corresponding background effective actions (see Eq. (
19
)), which explains in a simple and general way the equivalence observed when the background affine connection is on-shell. Based on this relation, we also argue that the counterterm Lagrangians in both formulations should coincide under the same condition. These features are explicitly verified at one-loop order in Sec. III, using a general background gauge. A brief discussion of the results is presented in Sec. IV . Some useful details of the computations and additional results are given in the Appendices.

II
Equivalence of formulations in the EH theory

The Einstein-Hilbert Lagrangian in the second order formulation can be written in the form
Goldberg 1958

\mathcal{L}^{\text{II}}(h)=-\frac{1}{\kappa^{2}}\sqrt{-g}g^{\mu\nu}R_{\mu\nu}(\Gamma)\equiv-\frac{1}{\kappa^{2}}h^{\mu\nu}R_{\mu\nu}(\Gamma)

(1)

where
\kappa^{2}=16\pi G_{N}
(
G_{N}
is Newton’s gravitational constant),
g=\det g_{\mu\nu}
, the Goldberg metric
h^{\mu\nu}=\sqrt{-g}g^{\mu\nu}
and the Ricci tensor is given by

R_{\mu\nu}(\Gamma)=\Gamma_{\mu\lambda}{}^{\lambda}_{,\nu}-\Gamma_{\mu\nu}{}^{\lambda}_{,\lambda}-\Gamma_{\mu\nu}{}^{\lambda}\Gamma_{\lambda\sigma}{}^{\sigma}+\Gamma_{\mu\lambda}{}^{\sigma}\Gamma_{\nu\sigma}{}^{\lambda},

(2)

where a comma denote a partial derivative.
The Levi-Civita connection is expressed in terms of the metric as

\Gamma_{\mu\nu}{}^{\lambda}=\frac{1}{2}g^{\lambda\rho}(g_{\mu\rho,\nu}+g_{\nu\rho,\mu}-g_{\mu\nu,\rho}).

(3)

In the background field method, one splits the gauge field
h^{\mu\nu}
as
\bar{h}^{\mu\nu}+\kappa\mathfrak{h}^{\mu\nu}
, where
\bar{h}^{\mu\nu}
and
\mathfrak{h}^{\mu\nu}
denote respectively the background and quantum parts of this field.
Then, a term is added which breaks the gauge invariance of the quantum field. A convenient gauge-fixing Lagrangian
\mathcal{L}_{\text{gf}}
, which is dependent upon the background field
\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}
, may be chosen as

\mathcal{L}_{\text{gf}}=-\frac{1}{2\xi}\bar{h}_{\nu\beta}(\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\mu}\mathfrak{h}^{\mu\nu})(\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\alpha}\mathfrak{h}^{\alpha\beta})

(4)

where
\xi
is a general gauge fixing parameter and
\bar{\mathsf{D}}
is the background covariant derivative.
We note that
\mathcal{L}_{\text{gf}}
leads to a ghost Lagrangian induced by the Faddeev-Popov determinant

\begin{split}\Delta_{\text{FP}}(\bar{{h}})=\int\mathop{\mathcal{D}\bar{c}^{\mu}}\mathop{\mathcal{D}c^{\nu}}\exp-i\int\mathop{d^{{4}}x}\sqrt{-\bar{h}}\bar{h}_{\beta\nu}c^{\star\beta}\left[\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\mu}\left(\bar{h}^{\mu\lambda}\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\lambda}c^{\nu}+\bar{h}^{\lambda\nu}\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\lambda}c^{\mu}-\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\lambda}(\bar{h}^{\mu\nu}c^{\lambda})\right)\right],\end{split}

(5)

where
\bar{h}\equiv\det\bar{h}^{\mu\nu}={\bar{g}}
.
The above Lagrangians are invariant under the gauge transformations

\displaystyle\Delta\bar{{h}}^{\mu\nu}={}

\displaystyle\bar{h}^{\lambda\nu}\partial_{\lambda}\zeta^{\mu}+\bar{h}^{\mu\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}\zeta^{\nu}-\partial_{\lambda}(\bar{h}^{\mu\nu}\zeta^{\lambda}),\quad\Delta\mathfrak{h}^{\mu\nu}=\mathfrak{h}^{\lambda\nu}\partial_{\lambda}\zeta^{\mu}+\mathfrak{h}^{\mu\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}\zeta^{\nu}-\partial_{\lambda}(\mathfrak{h}^{\mu\nu}\zeta^{\lambda});\quad

(6)

where
\zeta^{\mu}
is an arbitrary infinitesimal parameter.

The first order Lagrangian has an identical form to that in Eq. (
1
), but now
h^{\mu\nu}
and

are treated as independent fields. In this case, it is convenient to use, instead of

(7)

In terms of this field, the corresponding first order Lagrangian may be written as

(10)

This form was obtained by omitting the terms linear in the quantum fields, which can propagate only inside the loops. Such a procedure yields the proper one-particle irreducible Green functions, when the background fields are off-shell
Abbott 1981
;
Weinberg 2000
;
Frenkel and Taylor 2018
.

(13)

The Eq. (
12
) shows that when the background field
\bar{G}
is on-shell, the first and the second order Lagrangians in the background field method effectively become equivalent. Such a result indicates that in this case, the effective actions at one-loop order may also become equal.

This statement can be established by deriving basic relations between the generating functionals of Green’s functions in the presence of background fields. To that end, we observe that, in the first order formulation, the functional takes the form

\bar{Z}^{\text{I}}[j,J;\bar{\mathfrak{h}},\bar{G}]=\int\mathop{\mathcal{D}\mathfrak{h}^{\mu\nu}}\mathop{\mathcal{D}\mathchoice{\mathfrak{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathfrak{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathfrak{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathfrak{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}}\Delta_{\text{FP}}(\bar{\mathfrak{h}})\exp i\int\mathop{d^{{4}}x}\left[\mathcal{L}^{\text{I}}(\bar{{h}},\mathfrak{h};\bar{G},\mathfrak{G})+\mathcal{L}_{\text{gf}}+j_{\mu\nu}\mathfrak{h}^{\mu\nu}+\mathchoice{J^{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{J^{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{J^{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{J^{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}\mathchoice{\mathfrak{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85 (14)

where we define

,

(15)

Using the Eqs. (

)–(

), setting

on shell, we get the relation

(16)

A similar equation holds for the generating functionals of connected Green functions:
\bar{W}=-i\ln{\bar{Z}}
.

(17a)

(17b)

Here, the mean quantum fields
\hat{\tilde{\mathfrak{h}}}^{\mu\nu}
,

and

are defined by

\hat{\tilde{\mathfrak{h}}}^{\mu\nu}=\frac{\delta\bar{W}^{\text{II}}}{\delta j_{\mu\nu}},\quad\hat{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}=\frac{\delta\bar{W}^{\text{I}}}{\delta (18)

Of special interest is the background effective action, obtained by evaluating
\bar{\Gamma}
for vanishing mean quantum fields. Thus, putting
J
to zero, setting the field
\mathchoice{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
on-shell and using Eqs. (
13
), (
16
) and Eq. (
17
), one obtains the basic relation

\bar{\Gamma}^{\text{I}}[0,0;\bar{\mathfrak{h}},\mathcal{G}(\bar{\mathfrak{{h}}})]=\bar{\Gamma}^{\text{II}}[0;\bar{\mathfrak{h}}].

(19)

This equation directly demonstrates the equivalence at the quantum level of the two formulations of the Einstein-Hilbert theory. As discussed in Appendix
B
, an analogous result also holds in Yang–Mills theory. In the following section, we verify Eq. (
19
) explicitly at one-loop order in a general background gauge.

III
One-Loop Counterterms in a General Background Gauge

The one-loop diagrams contributing in the first order formulation to the background self-energies
\bar{\mathfrak{h}}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}
,
\bar{G}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}
and
\bar{G}\bar{G}
are shown in Fig.
1
, where we have defined:
\bar{h}^{\mu\nu}=\eta^{\mu\nu}+\kappa\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}
.

Figure 1:
Diagrams that contributes to the self-energies
\bar{\mathfrak{h}}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}
(a),
\bar{G}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}
(b) and
\bar{G}\bar{G}
(c) in the first order formulation of the EH theory. Wavy and solid lines represents respectively the background fields
\bar{\mathfrak{h}}
and
{\bar{G}}
. The quantum fields
\mathfrak{h}
and
\mathfrak{G}
are represented by springy and double solid lines. Momenta in the loops flow clockwise, so that,
q=p+k
.

At one-loop order, the two-point function can be written in terms of these self-energies as

\mathcal{L}^{\text{I}}_{(2)}(\bar{\mathfrak{h}},\bar{G})=\frac{1}{2}\left(\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\alpha\beta}+2\mathchoice{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{\Pi^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Pi^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Pi^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Pi^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}{\mu\nu}}}}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}+\mathchoice{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{\Pi^{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}}{\Pi^{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}}{\Pi^{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}}{\Pi^{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}}\mathchoice{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\right),

(20)

where both background fields
\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}
and
\mathchoice{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
are off-shell. As shown in Eqs. (
79
) and (
80
), the above self-energies satisfy Ward identities that are induced by the background gauge invariance of the EH theory under the background field transformations (
11
).
In a general background gauge, the Eq. (
20
) leads to a very involved expression depending upon the gauge parameter
\xi
, which has been evaluated in Appendix
A
.

Here, we discuss only the result obtained by setting the background field
\mathchoice{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
on-shell (see Eq. (
13
)), which is needed for the verification of Eq. (
19
). In this case, Eq. (
20
) yields the proper background graviton self-energy in the first order formulation.
This self-energy may be expressed in terms of five independent tensors built from
\eta_{\mu\nu}
and
k_{\alpha}
, as

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{(1)}{}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}(k)={}

\displaystyle\frac{k_{\mu}k_{\nu}k_{\alpha}k_{\beta}}{k^{4}},

(21a)

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{(2)}{}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}(k)={}

\displaystyle\eta_{\mu\nu}\eta_{\alpha\beta},

(21b)

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{(3)}{}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}(k)={}

\displaystyle\eta_{\mu\alpha}\eta_{\nu\beta}+\eta_{\mu\beta}\eta_{\nu\alpha},

(21c)

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{(4)}{}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}(k)={}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{k^{2}}\left(k_{\mu}k_{\nu}\eta_{\alpha\beta}+k_{\alpha}k_{\beta}\eta_{\mu\nu}\right),

(21d)

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{(5)}{}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}(k)={}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{k^{2}}\left(k_{\mu}k_{\alpha}\eta_{\nu\beta}+k_{\nu}k_{\alpha}\eta_{\mu\beta}+k_{\mu}k_{\beta}\eta_{\nu\alpha}+k_{\nu}k_{\beta}\eta_{\mu\alpha}\right).

(21e)

Thus, we can write the divergent part of this self-energy in
{{D}}=4-2\epsilon
dimensions, in the form

\Pi^{\text{I div}}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}(k)=\frac{\kappa^{2}k^{4}}{16\pi^{2}\epsilon}\sum_{i=1}^{5}C_{i}(\xi)\mathcal{T}^{(i)}{}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}(k),

(22)

where the coefficients
C_{i}(\xi)
are dimensionless functions of the gauge parameter.

The proper background graviton self-energy satisfies the Ward identity

(\eta^{\mu\rho}k^{\nu}+\eta^{\nu\rho}k^{\mu}-\eta^{\mu\nu}k^{\rho})\Pi^{\text{I}}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}(k)=0,

(23)

which is a consequence of gauge invariance under the background field transformations (
6
). The above identity implies that the coefficients
C_{i}
are not independent. Indeed, one finds after an explicit calculation, the relations

\displaystyle C_{1}(\xi)={}

\displaystyle 4\left[C_{2}(\xi)+C_{3}(\xi)\right],

(24a)

\displaystyle C_{2}(\xi)={}

\displaystyle-\frac{20\xi+99}{240},

(24b)

\displaystyle C_{3}(\xi)={}

\displaystyle\frac{20(\xi-1)\xi+87}{80},

(24c)

\displaystyle C_{4}(\xi)={}

\displaystyle 2\left[C_{2}(\xi)+C_{3}(\xi)\right],

(24d)

\displaystyle C_{5}(\xi)={}

\displaystyle-C_{3}(\xi).

(24e)

We can connect the self-energy (
22
) to the counterterm Lagrangian by using the relations

\displaystyle\bar{R}_{\mu\nu}={}

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa}{2}\left[k_{\mu}k_{\nu}L_{\rho\sigma}-\frac{k^{2}}{2}\left(L_{\mu\sigma}L_{\rho\nu}+L_{\mu\rho}L_{\sigma\nu}\right)\right]\left(\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\rho\sigma}\mathchoice{\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{{{\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.68787pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.68787pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.14204pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.53003pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.68787pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.68787pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.14204pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.53003pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\tau}}}}{\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{{{\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.68787pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.68787pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.14204pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.53003pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.68787pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.68787pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.14204pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.53003pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\tau}}}}{\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{{{\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.68787pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.68787pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.14204pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.53003pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.68787pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.68787pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.14204pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.53003pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\tau}}}}{\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{{{\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.68787pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.68787pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.14204pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.53003pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.68787pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.68787pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.14204pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.53003pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\tau}}}}-\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\rho\sigma}\right)+O(\kappa^{2}),

(25a)

\displaystyle\bar{R}={}

\displaystyle\kappa k^{2}L_{\alpha\beta}\left(\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{{{\sigma}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}}{\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{{{\sigma}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}}{\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{{{\sigma}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}}{\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{{{\sigma}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}}-\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\alpha\beta}\right)+O(\kappa^{2}),

(25b)

where
\bar{R}_{\mu\nu}
is the Ricci tensor,
\bar{R}
is the curvature scalar and
L_{\mu\nu}(k)=k_{\mu}k_{\nu}/k^{2}-\eta_{\mu\nu}
. In this way, we obtain for the graviton background counterterm Lagrangian, the result

\left.\mathcal{L}_{\text{CT}}^{\text{I}}\right|_{\bar{G}=\mathcal{G}}=\frac{\sqrt{-\bar{g}}}{16\pi^{2}\epsilon}\left[-\left(\frac{\xi-1}{6}+\frac{119}{120}\right)\bar{R}^{2}+\left(\xi(\xi-1)+\frac{87}{20}\right)\bar{R}_{\mu\nu}\bar{R}^{\mu\nu}\right].

(26)

For
\xi=1
, Eq. (
26
) reduces to the result obtained in Ref.
Gonçalves et al. 2018
in the Goldberg parametrization (see Appendix
D
), providing an explicit one-loop verification of the equivalence in Eq. (
19
).
This equivalence further implies that Eq. (
26
) yields the general counterterm Lagrangian of the second-order EH theory for arbitrary gauge parameter
\xi
.

IV
Discussion

We have investigated the equivalence between the first and second order formulations of the Einstein–Hilbert theory in a general background gauge. As a key step, we derived Eq. (
16
), which relates the generating functionals of connected Green’s functions in the two formulations. This relation leads directly to Eq. (
19
), establishing that the corresponding background effective actions coincide at the quantum level when the background affine connection is on-shell. Notably, this equivalence remains valid even when the background graviton field is off-shell.
The basic relation (
19
) provides, to our knowledge, a novel and direct proof for the equivalence of the background effective actions in the two formulations.

The expression (
19
) has been verified through explicit calculations of the divergent part of the background metric and affine connections
self-energies at one loop order. These functions have been computed in a general background gauge, with both background fields taken off-shell. We have shown that these self-energies satisfy Ward identities which reflect the diffeomorphism invariance of the EH theory.
Such explicit calculations are useful as these may ascertain and clarify the formal arguments for the equivalence at the quantum level, of the first and second order formulations of the Einstein-Hilbert theory.

In some theories, the original fields may not be the optimal variables to describe the physical content of the models. On the other hand, a change of variables could lead to enhanced physical insights. This feature also occurs in the first order formulation of the EH theory
Brandt and McKeon 2016
. In this case, it is useful to make the change of variables

\mathchoice{G^{{{\prime}\kern 6.56604pt{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}}}}{G^{{{\prime}\kern 6.56604pt{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}}}}{G^{{{\prime}\kern 5.60423pt{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}}}}{G^{{{\prime}\kern 5.60423pt{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}}}}=\mathchoice{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}+\mathchoice{(M^{-1})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(M^{-1})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(M^{-1})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(M^{-1})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][ [c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{,\sigma}}}},

(27)

where
h^{\alpha\beta}
is the graviton field, the affine connection field
\mathchoice{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
is defined in Eq. (
7
) and the tensor
M
is given in Eq. (
9
).
Then, it turns out that the generating functional of Green’s functions containing only external graviton fields, directly reduces to the appropriate generating functional in the second order EH theory.

We point out here that the equivalence expressed in Eq. (
19
) is not a forthright consequence of the Kallosh–DeWitt theorem
DeWitt 1964
;
DeWitt 1967
;
Kallosh 1974
, which guarantees the equality of S-matrix elements, as our analysis allows for an off-shell background graviton field. A natural extension of this work would be to consider the Einstein–Cartan theory, which is structurally more complex due to the presence of two gauge symmetries: diffeomorphism invariance and local Lorentz invariance
Brandt et al. 2024a
;
Brandt et al. 2024b
. This generalization is currently being examined.

Appendix A
Background self-energies in the first-order Einstein-Hilbert theory

In this appendix, we will show the details for the computation of the divergent part of the self-energies
\bar{\mathfrak{h}}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}
,
\bar{G}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}
and
\bar{G}\bar{G}
of the first order EH theory.
In order to compute it, we adopt the Passarino–Veltman approach
Passarino and Veltman 1979
(for instance, see Appendix B of Ref.
Brandt et al. 2022
), which systematically reduces tensorial loop integrals to scalar integrals. We will use dimensional regularization
Leibbrandt 1975
with
{{D}}=4-2\epsilon
, where
\epsilon
is taken to be a small parameter.

In this method, the self-energies are expanded in terms of appropriate tensorial bases. Therefore, a suitable basis must be specified for each type of tensor structure that appears. In addition to the basis introduced in Eq. (
21
) for the graviton self-energy, we will also employ the following basis for tensor structures of the form:

•

\langle G^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}h^{\pi\tau}\rangle
:

\displaystyle(\mathcal{T}^{Gh}_{1})^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\pi\tau}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{4}(k^{\pi}\delta^{\lambda}_{\nu}\delta_{\mu}^{\tau}+k^{\pi}\delta^{\lambda}_{\mu}\delta_{\nu}^{\tau}+\delta^{\pi}_{\nu}k^{\tau}\delta^{\lambda}_{\mu}+\delta^{\pi}_{\mu}k^{\tau}\delta^{\lambda}_{\nu}),

(28a)

\displaystyle(\mathcal{T}^{Gh}_{2})^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\pi\tau}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{2}k^{\lambda}(\delta^{\pi}_{\nu}\delta_{\mu}^{\tau}+\delta^{\pi}_{\mu}\delta_{\nu}^{\tau}),

(28b)

\displaystyle(\mathcal{T}^{Gh}_{3})^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\pi\tau}

\displaystyle=k^{\lambda}\eta^{\pi\tau}\eta_{\mu\nu},

(28c)

\displaystyle(\mathcal{T}^{Gh}_{4})^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\pi\tau}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{2}\eta_{\mu\nu}(k^{\pi}\eta^{\lambda\tau}+\eta^{\lambda\pi}k^{\tau}),

(28d)

\displaystyle(\mathcal{T}^{Gh}_{5})^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\pi\tau}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{4}(\delta^{\pi}_{\nu}k_{\mu}\eta^{\lambda\tau}+\eta^{\lambda\pi}k_{\mu}\delta_{\nu}^{\tau}+\delta^{\pi}_{\mu}k_{\nu}\eta^{\lambda\tau}+\eta^{\lambda\pi}k_{\nu}\delta_{\mu}^{\tau}),

(28e)

\displaystyle(\mathcal{T}^{Gh}_{6})^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\pi\tau}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{2}\eta^{\pi\tau}(k_{\mu}\delta^{\lambda}_{\nu}+k_{\nu}\delta^{\lambda}_{\mu}),

(28f)

\displaystyle(\mathcal{T}^{Gh}_{7})^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\pi\tau}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{2k^{2}}k^{\pi}k^{\tau}(k_{\mu}\delta^{\lambda}_{\nu}+k_{\nu}\delta^{\lambda}_{\mu}),

(28g)

\displaystyle(\mathcal{T}^{Gh}_{8})^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\pi\tau}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{4k^{2}}k^{\lambda}(k^{\pi}k_{\mu}\delta_{\nu}^{\tau}+k^{\pi}k_{\nu}\delta_{\mu}^{\tau}+\delta^{\pi}_{\nu}k_{\mu}k^{\tau}+\delta^{\pi}_{\mu}k_{\nu}k^{\tau}),

(28h)

\displaystyle(\mathcal{T}^{Gh}_{9})^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\pi\tau}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{2k^{2}}k_{\mu}k_{\nu}(k^{\pi}\eta^{\lambda\tau}+\eta^{\lambda\pi}k^{\tau}),

(28i)

\displaystyle(\mathcal{T}^{Gh}_{10})^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\pi\tau}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{k^{4}}k^{\lambda}k_{\mu}k_{\nu}k^{\pi}k^{\tau},

(28j)

\displaystyle(\mathcal{T}^{Gh}_{11})^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\pi\tau}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{k^{2}}k^{\lambda}\eta_{\mu\nu}k^{\pi}k^{\tau},

(28k)

\displaystyle(\mathcal{T}^{Gh}_{12})^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\pi\tau}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{k^{2}}k^{\lambda}k_{\mu}k_{\nu}\eta^{\pi\tau}.

(28l)

•

\langle G_{\mu\nu}^{\lambda}G_{\pi\tau}^{\rho}\rangle
:

\displaystyle(\mathcal{T}^{GG}_{1})^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\rho}_{\pi\tau}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{4}(\delta_{\pi}^{\rho}\delta^{\lambda}_{\nu}\eta_{\mu\tau}+\delta_{\pi}^{\rho}\delta^{\lambda}_{\mu}\eta_{\nu\tau}+\eta_{\pi\nu}\delta^{\lambda}_{\mu}\delta^{\rho}_{\tau}+\eta_{\pi\mu}\delta^{\lambda}_{\nu}\delta^{\rho}_{\tau}),

(29a)

\displaystyle(\mathcal{T}^{GG}_{2})^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\rho}_{\pi\tau}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{2}\eta^{\lambda\rho}(\eta_{\pi\nu}\eta_{\mu\tau}+\eta_{\pi\mu}\eta_{\nu\tau}),

(29b)

\displaystyle(\mathcal{T}^{GG}_{3})^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\rho}_{\pi\tau}

\displaystyle=\eta_{\pi\tau}\eta^{\lambda\rho}\eta_{\mu\nu},

(29c)

\displaystyle(\mathcal{T}^{GG}_{4})^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\rho}_{\pi\tau}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{4}(\eta_{\pi\nu}\delta^{\lambda}_{\tau}\delta_{\mu}^{\rho}+\delta_{\pi}^{\lambda}\delta_{\mu}^{\rho}\eta_{\nu\tau}+\eta_{\pi\mu}\delta^{\lambda}_{\tau}\delta_{\nu}^{\rho}+\delta_{\pi}^{\lambda}\eta_{\mu\tau}\delta_{\nu}^{\rho}),

(29d)

\displaystyle(\mathcal{T}^{GG}_{5})^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\rho}_{\pi\tau}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{4}(\delta_{\pi}^{\rho}\delta^{\lambda}_{\tau}\eta_{\mu\nu}+\delta_{\pi}^{\lambda}\eta_{\mu\nu}\delta^{\rho}_{\tau}+\eta_{\pi\tau}\delta^{\lambda}_{\nu}\delta_{\mu}^{\rho}+\eta_{\pi\tau}\delta^{\lambda}_{\mu}\delta_{\nu}^{\rho}),

(29e)

(29f)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{4k^{4}}k^{\lambda}k^{\rho}(k_{\pi}k_{\mu}\eta_{\nu\tau}+k_{\pi}k_{\nu}\eta_{\mu\tau}+\eta_{\pi\nu}k_{\mu}k_{\tau}+\eta_{\pi\mu}k_{\nu}k_{\tau}),

A.1
Feynman rules

The Feynman rules can be obtained from the BFM effective action:

\frac{1}{2}\mathchoice{\mathfrak{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathfrak{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathfrak{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathfrak{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{M^{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt \nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.78293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.78293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.3333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.80948pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}}}}(\mathfrak{h})\mathchoice{\mathfrak{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.78293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.78293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.3333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.80948pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}}}_{{{\sigma\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathfrak{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.78293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.78293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.3333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.80948pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}}}_{{{\sigma\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathfrak{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.78293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.78293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.3333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.80948pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}}}_{{{\sigma\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathfrak{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.78293pt][c] {\makebox[8.37271pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{,\lambda}}}}{\mathfrak{h}^{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.37271pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.37271pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{,\lambda}}}}{\mathfrak{h}^{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.37271pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.37271pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{,\lambda}}}}{\mathfrak{h}^{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.37271pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.37271pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{,\lambda}}}}+\mathfrak{L}_{\text{gh}}(h)+\mathfrak{L}_{\text{gh}}(h),

(30)

where

\mathfrak{L}_{\text{gf}}(h)=-\frac{1}{2\xi}\bar{h}_{\nu\beta}\left[\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\mu}\mathfrak{h}^{\mu\nu}\right]\left[\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\alpha}\mathfrak{h}^{\alpha\beta}\right],

(31)

where
\mathfrak{h}=\mathfrak{h}^{\mu\nu}\bar{g}_{\mu\nu}
,
and

\mathfrak{L}_{\text{gh}}(h)=-\sqrt{-\bar{h}}\bar{h}_{\beta\nu}c^{\star\beta}\left[\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\mu}\left(\bar{h}^{\mu\lambda}\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\lambda}c^{\nu}+\bar{h}^{\lambda\nu}\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\lambda}c^{\mu}-\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\lambda}(\bar{h}^{\mu\nu}c^{\lambda})\right)\right].

(32)

Eq. (
32
) follows from the BRST form of the ghost action:

-\sqrt{-\bar{h}}\bar{h}_{\beta\nu}d^{\star\beta}\left[\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\mu}\left(\bar{h}^{\mu\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}d^{\nu}+\bar{h}^{\lambda\nu}\partial_{\lambda}d^{\mu}-\partial_{\lambda}(\bar{h}^{\mu\nu}d^{\lambda})\right)\right].

(33)

It can be written in a more familiar form as

-\sqrt{-\bar{h}}c^{\star\mu}\left[\bar{h}_{\mu\nu}\bar{h}^{\gamma\lambda}\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\lambda}\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\gamma}-\bar{R}_{\nu\mu}(\bar{G})+\mathsf{X}_{\mu\nu}\right]c^{\nu},

(34)

where

R_{\nu\mu}=-{G}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\lambda}{}_{,\lambda}-\frac{1}{{{D}}-1}\left({G}_{\mu\lambda}{}^{\lambda}{G}_{\nu\sigma}{}^{\sigma}+\partial_{\nu}\mathchoice{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.49626pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.49626pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.75241pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.10886pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\mu\sigma}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.49626pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.49626pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.75241pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.10886pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\mu\sigma}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.49626pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.49626pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.75241pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.10886pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\mu\sigma}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.49626pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.49626pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.75241pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.10886pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\mu\sigma}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}-\partial_{\mu}\mathchoice{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.64322pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.64322pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.22044pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.72887pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\nu\sigma}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.64322pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.64322pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.22044pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.72887pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\nu\sigma}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.64322pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.64322pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.22044pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.72887pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\nu\sigma}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.64322pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.64322pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.22044pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.72887pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\nu\sigma}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\right)+{G}_{\mu\sigma}{}^{\lambda}{G}_{\nu\lambda}{}^{\sigma}

(35)

and

\mathsf{X}_{\mu\nu}=\bar{g}_{\mu\nu}\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\rho}\bar{h}^{\rho\lambda}\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\lambda}-\bar{g}_{\mu\lambda}(\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\rho}\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\nu}\bar{h}^{\rho\lambda})\mathds{1}-\bar{g}_{\mu\lambda}\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\rho}\bar{h}^{\rho\lambda}\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\nu}.

(36)

When metricity
D_{\mu}g_{\alpha\beta}=0
is assumed,
\mathsf{X}_{\mu\nu}=0
and
R_{\nu\mu}=R_{\mu\nu}
and Eq. (
34
) reduces to the form found in ’t Hooft and Veltman
’t Hooft and Veltman 1974
.

To obtain Eq. (
34
), we used that

\bar{h}_{\beta\nu}c^{\star\beta}\left[\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\mu}\left(\bar{h}^{\mu\lambda}\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\lambda}c^{\nu}+\bar{h}^{\lambda\nu}\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\lambda}c^{\mu}-\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\lambda}(\bar{h}^{\mu\nu}c^{\lambda})\right)\right]=c^{\star\mu}\left(\bar{h}_{\mu\nu}\bar{h}^{\gamma\lambda}\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\lambda}\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\gamma}+[\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\nu},\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\mu}]+\mathsf{X}_{\mu\nu}\right)c^{\nu}

(37)

and

[\mathsf{D}_{\nu},\mathsf{D}_{\mu}]c^{\nu}=-R{}^{\nu}{}_{\beta}{}_{\nu\mu}c^{\beta}=-R_{\nu\mu}c^{\nu}.

(38)

The covariant derivative of a tensor in the first order formulation

\mathsf{D}_{\mu}\mathchoice{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}=\partial_{\mu}\mathchoice{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displ (40)

where we used the inverse of Eq. (
7
):

\Gamma_{\mu\nu}{}^{\lambda}=G_{\mu\nu}{}^{\lambda}+\frac{1}{1-{{D}}}\left(\delta^{\lambda}_{\mu}G_{\sigma\nu}{}^{\sigma}+\delta^{\lambda}_{\nu}G_{\sigma\mu}{}^{\sigma}\right).

(41)

A.1.1
Propagators

The propagators are the same as those found in the conventional formulation (see Ref.
Brandt and McKeon 2016
). They are obtained from the inverse of the bilinear terms in the quantum fields in Eq. (
30
):

\begin{bmatrix}\bm{A}&\bm{B}\\
\bm{C}&\bm{D}\end{bmatrix}\equiv\begin{bmatrix}-\frac{1}{4\xi}\left(k^{\tau}\left(k^{\rho}\eta^{\pi\sigma}+k^{\sigma}\eta^{\pi\rho}\right)+k^{\pi}\left(k^{\rho}\eta^{\tau\sigma}+k^{\sigma}\eta^{\rho\tau}\right)\right)&-ik^{\gamma}\left(\eta^{\pi\beta}\eta^{\alpha\tau}+\eta^{\pi\alpha}\eta^{\tau\beta}\right)\\
ik^{\lambda}\left(\eta^{\mu\sigma}\eta^{\rho\nu}+\eta^{\mu\rho}\eta^{\nu\sigma}\right)&\mathchoice{M^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{M^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{M^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox (42)

in momentum space
i\partial_{\mu}=k_{\mu}
. The inverse can be computed using the expression:

\begin{bmatrix}\bm{A}&\bm{B}\\
\bm{C}&\bm{D}\end{bmatrix}^{-1}=\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}^{-1}&-\bm{X}^{-1}\bm{B}\bm{D}^{-1}\\
-\bm{D}^{-1}\bm{C}\bm{X}^{-1}&\bm{D}^{-1}+\bm{D}^{-1}\bm{C}\bm{X}^{-1}\bm{B}\bm{D}^{-1}\end{pmatrix},

(43)

where
\bm{X}=\bm{A}-\bm{B}\bm{D}^{-1}\bm{C}
is the Schur complement. The inverse of
\bm{D}
is given by:

\begin{split}\mathchoice{(M^{-1})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\pi\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(M^{-1})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\pi\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(M^{-1})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textst &-\frac{1}{4}\left(h_{\tau\mu}\delta_{\nu}^{\rho}\delta_{\pi}^{\lambda}+h_{\pi\mu}\delta_{\nu}^{\rho}\delta_{\tau}^{\lambda}+h_{\tau\nu}\delta_{\mu}^{\rho}\delta_{\pi}^{\lambda}+h_{\pi\nu}\delta_{\mu}^{\rho}\delta_{\tau}^{\lambda}\right).\end{split}

thchoice{\makebox[8.78293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.78293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.3333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.80948pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho\sigma}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{M^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.78293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.78293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.3333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.80948pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho\sigma}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{M^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.78293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.78293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.3333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.80948pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho\sigma}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{M^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.78293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.78293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.3333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.80948pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho\sigma}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}(h)}{\delta h^{\pi\tau}}\\
={}&\frac{1}{8}\left\{\left[\left(\frac{\delta^{\gamma}_{\rho}\delta^{\pi}_{\nu}\delta^{\tau}_{\sigma}\delta^{\lambda}_{\mu}}{{{D}}-1}-\delta^{\lambda}_{\rho}\delta^{\pi}_{\nu}\delta^{\tau}_{\sigma}\delta^{\gamma}_{\mu}+\pi\leftrightarrow\tau\right)+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\right]+\rho\leftrightarrow\sigma\right\},\end{split}

(50)

where
\mu\leftrightarrow\nu
denotes an index permutation.
Note that, these vertices are momenta independent.

Now, we can consider the interactions that arises from the Faddeev-Popov action, which are momenta dependent.
First, we have the gauge-fixing Lagrangian (
31
). The interaction
\bar{\mathfrak{h}}\mathfrak{h}\mathfrak{h}
comes from the partial derivatives:

-\frac{\kappa}{2\xi}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}_{\nu\beta}\partial_{\mu}\mathfrak{h}^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\alpha}\mathfrak{h}^{\alpha\beta}.

(51)

This leads to the following vertex (all momenta flows inwards):

\vbox{\hbox{\includegraphics[scale]{vertexbhhh.eps}}}\begin{aligned} -\frac{i\kappa}{8\xi}&\big[p_{3}^{\alpha}\left[\eta^{\nu\beta}\left(p_{2}^{\pi}\eta^{\mu\tau}+p_{2}^{\tau}\eta^{\mu\pi}\right)+\eta^{\mu\beta}\left(p_{2}^{\pi}\eta^{\nu\tau}+p_{2}^{\tau}\eta^{\pi\nu}\right)\right]\\
&-p_{3}^{\beta}\left[p_{2}^{\pi}\left(\eta^{\mu\tau}\eta^{\alpha\nu}+\eta^{\mu\alpha}\eta^{\nu\tau}\right)+p_{2}^{\tau}\left(\eta^{\mu\alpha}\eta^{\pi\nu}+\eta^{\mu\pi}\eta^{\alpha\nu}\right)\right]\big].\end{aligned}

(52)

From the covariant derivatives (we have set
{{D}}=4
)

\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\mu}\mathfrak{h}^{\mu\nu}=\partial_{\mu}\mathfrak{h}^{\mu\mu}+\bar{G}{}_{\mu\rho}{}^{\mu}\mathfrak{h}^{\mu\rho}-\frac{2}{3}\bar{G}_{\lambda\rho}{}^{\lambda}\mathfrak{h}^{\rho\mu},

(53)

we get the interaction terms such as
\bar{G}\mathfrak{h}\mathfrak{h}
. The last term in Eq. (
53
) appears due to the tensor density nature of the Goldberg metric.
These terms yields the vertex

\vbox{\hbox{\includegraphics[scale]{vertexbGpp.eps}}}\begin{aligned} -\frac{1}{4\xi}&\big\{(p_{2}^{\pi}\eta^{\lambda\tau}+\eta^{\pi\lambda}p_{2}^{\tau})(\delta^{\alpha}_{\nu}\delta^{\beta}_{\mu}+\delta^{\alpha}_{\mu}\delta^{\beta}_{\nu})\\
&\quad+(p_{3}^{\alpha}\eta^{\beta\lambda}+p_{3}^{\beta}\eta^{\alpha\lambda})(\delta^{\pi}_{\nu}\delta^{\tau}_{\mu}+\delta^{\pi}_{\mu}\delta^{\tau}_{\nu})\\
&-\frac{1}{3}\big[{p_{2}}^{\pi}(\eta^{\alpha\tau}\delta^{\beta}_{\nu}\delta_{\mu}^{\lambda}+\delta^{\alpha}_{\nu}\eta^{\beta\tau}\delta_{\mu}^{\lambda}+\eta^{\alpha\tau}\delta^{\beta}_{\mu}\delta^{\lambda}_{\nu}+\delta^{\alpha}_{\mu}\eta^{\beta\tau}\delta^{\lambda}_{\nu})\\
&\qquad+{p_{2}}^{\tau}(\eta^{\pi\beta}\delta^{\alpha}_{\nu}\delta_{\mu}^{\lambda}+\eta^{\pi\alpha}\delta^{\beta}_{\nu}\delta_{\mu}^{\lambda}+\eta^{\pi\beta}\delta^{\alpha}_{\mu}\delta^{\lambda}_{\nu}+\eta^{\pi\alpha}\delta^{\beta}_{\mu}\delta^{\lambda}_{\nu})\\
&\qquad+{p_{3}}^{\beta}(\delta^{\pi}_{\nu}\eta^{\alpha\tau}\delta_{\mu}^{\lambda}+\eta^{\pi\alpha}\delta_{\mu}^{\lambda}\delta^{\tau}_{\nu}+\delta^{\pi}_{\mu}\eta^{\alpha\tau}\delta^{\lambda}_{\nu}+\eta^{\pi\alpha}\delta^{\lambda}_{\nu}\delta^{\tau}_{\mu})\\
&\qquad+{p_{3}}^{\alpha}(\delta^{\pi}_{\nu}\eta^{\beta\tau}\delta_{\mu}^{\lambda}+\eta^{\pi\beta}\delta_{\mu}^{\lambda}\delta^{\tau}_{\nu}+\delta^{\pi}_{\mu}\eta^{\beta\tau}\delta^{\lambda}_{\nu}+\eta^{\pi\beta}\delta^{\lambda}_{\nu}\delta^{\tau}_{\mu})\big]\big\}.\end{aligned}

(54)

Finally, we have the interaction terms that comes from the ghost Lagrangian (
32
). From partial derivative only, we have the contributions

\kappa c^{\star\mu}\left[\eta_{\mu\nu}\left(\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{\beta}+\frac{1}{2}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}_{\rho}^{\rho}\partial_{\alpha}\partial^{\alpha}\right)+\bar{\mathfrak{h}}_{\mu\nu}\partial_{\alpha}\partial^{\alpha}\right]c^{\nu}+\kappa\eta_{\beta\nu}c^{\star\beta}\left[(\partial_{\mu}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\lambda})\partial_{\lambda}c^{\nu}-(\partial_{\mu}\partial_{\lambda}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu})c^{\lambda}-(\partial_{\mu}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu})\partial_{\lambda}c^{\lambda}\right],

(55)

where we used that
\sqrt{\bar{h}}=1+\kappa\bar{\mathfrak{h}}_{\rho}^{\rho}/2+O(\kappa^{2})
.
From the terms that arises from the covariant derivatives

\begin{split}\mathsf{D}_{\lambda}\left(\mathsf{D}_{\gamma}d^{\nu}\right)={}&\partial_{\lambda}\left\{\left[G_{\gamma\alpha}{}^{\nu}-\frac{1}{3}(\delta^{\nu}_{\gamma}G_{\sigma\alpha}{}^{\sigma}+\delta^{\nu}_{\alpha}G_{\sigma\gamma}{}^{\sigma})\right]d^{\alpha}\right\}\\
&+\left[G_{\lambda\alpha}{}^{\nu}-\frac{1}{3}\left(\delta^{\nu}_{\lambda}G_{\sigma\alpha}{}^{\sigma}+\delta^{\nu}_{\alpha}G_{\sigma\lambda}{}^{\sigma}\right)\right]\partial_{\gamma}d^{\alpha}\\
&-\left[G_{\lambda\gamma}{}^{\alpha}-\frac{1}{3}(\delta^{\alpha}_{\lambda}G_{\sigma\gamma}{}^{\sigma}+\delta^{\alpha}_{\gamma}G_{\sigma\lambda}{}^{\sigma})\right]\partial_{\alpha}d^{\nu}\end{split}

(56)

and the terms of the ghost action (
32
),
\bar{R}_{\nu\mu}(\bar{G})=-\mathchoice{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.37271pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.37271pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{,\lambda}}}}{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.37271pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.37271pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{,\lambda}}}}{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.37271pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.37271pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{,\lambda}}}}{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.37271pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.37271pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{,\lambda}}}}-(\mathchoice{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.15826pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.15826pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.95389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.53847pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.49196pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.49196pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.3136pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.08113pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\nu\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{,\mu}}}}{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.15826pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.15826pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.95389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.53847pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.49196pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.49196pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.3136pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.08113pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\nu\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{,\mu}}}}{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.15826pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.15826pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.95389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.53847pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.49196pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.49196pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.3136pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.08113pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\nu\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{,\mu}}}}{G^{{\math
