makebox[2.70114pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{,\nu}}}}{G^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.0113pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.0113pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.48586pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.91846pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.63892pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.63892pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.78163pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.70114pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{,\nu}}}})/3+O(\bar{G}^{2})
and
\mathsf{X}_{\mu\nu}
, we get all the contributions to the vertex
\bar{G}\mathfrak{h}\mathfrak{h}
.

Above interactions terms lead to the ghost vertices:

\vbox{\hbox{\includegraphics[scale]{vertexbpbcc.eps}}}\begin{aligned} \frac{i\kappa}{2}\big[&p_{1}^{\tau}(p_{3}^{\pi}\eta^{\nu\mu}-p_{1}^{\mu}\eta^{\pi\nu}-p_{3}^{\mu}\eta^{\pi\nu})-p_{1}^{\pi}(p_{1}^{\mu}\eta^{\nu\tau}+p_{3}^{\mu}\eta^{\nu\tau}-p_{3}^{\tau}\eta^{\nu\mu})\\
&+2p_{3}^{\pi}p_{3}^{\tau}\eta^{\nu\mu}+(p_{3})^{2}(\eta^{\pi\tau}\eta^{\nu\mu}-\eta^{\pi\mu}\eta^{\nu\tau}-\eta^{\pi\nu}\eta^{\mu\tau})\big]\end{aligned}

(57)

and

\vbox{\hbox{\includegraphics[scale]{vertexbGbcc.eps}}}\begin{aligned} \frac{1}{3}\big[&\delta_{\tau}^{\lambda}(p_{3}^{\pi}\eta_{\mu\nu}-\eta_{\pi\nu}p_{3}^{\mu}+\eta_{\pi\mu}p_{3}^{\nu})\\
&-3\delta_{\nu}^{\lambda}(p_{3}^{\pi}\eta_{\mu\tau}-\eta_{\pi\tau}p_{3}^{\mu}+\eta_{\pi\mu}p_{3}^{\tau})\\
&-\delta_{\pi}^{\lambda}(p_{3}^{\mu}\eta_{\nu\tau}-p_{3}^{\nu}\eta_{\mu\tau}-p_{3}^{\tau}\eta_{\mu\nu})\big].\end{aligned}

(58)

Quartic vertex are omitted, since they lead only to tadpoles-like contributions, which in dimensional regularization vanish.

A.2
Self-Energies

Using the Feynman rules obtained above, we can compute the one-loop diagrams in Fig.
1
(a) using the Passarino-Veltman approach. The divergent part of the self-energies
\bar{\mathfrak{h}}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}
,
\bar{G}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}
,
\bar{\mathfrak{h}}\bar{G}
and
\bar{G}\bar{G}
reads

\displaystyle\Pi_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}(k)={}

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa^{2}k^{4}}{16\pi^{2}\epsilon}\sum_{J=I}^{V}\sum_{i=1}^{5}a_{i}^{(J)}(\xi)\mathcal{T}^{(i)}{}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}(k),

(59a)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\Pi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\pi\tau}}}}{\Pi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\pi\tau}}}}{\Pi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\pi\tau}}}}{\Pi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\m The coefficients
b_{10}
,
c_{6}
–
c_{9}
,
c_{20}
and
c_{21}
vanish, since non-local terms, as

k^{2}\frac{k_{\mu}k_{\nu}k_{\alpha}k_{\beta}}{k^{4}}\xrightarrow[\text{space}]{\text{position}}\frac{\partial_{\mu}\partial_{\nu}\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{\beta}}{\partial^{2}}\quad\text{and}\quad k^{2}\frac{k_{\mu}k_{\nu}k_{\alpha}k_{\beta}k^{\lambda}k^{\rho}\partial^{\lambda}\partial^{\rho}}{k^{6}}\xrightarrow[\text{space}]{\text{position}}\frac{\partial_{\mu}\partial_{\nu}\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{\beta}}{\partial^{2}\partial^{2}},

(60)

would arise. Such terms are forbidden due to the locality of gauge transformation and the corresponding BRST transformations.

A.3
Proper self-energy

Setting the independent field
\bar{G}
on-shell in Eq. (
20
), we obtain the proper effective action

\Gamma^{\text{I}}\left(\bar{\mathfrak{h}},\bar{G}(\bar{\mathfrak{h})}\right)=\frac{1}{2}\left(\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\alpha\beta}+2\mathchoice{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{\Pi^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8 pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}{\mu\nu}}}}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}+\mathchoice{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{\Pi^{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}}{\Pi^{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}}{\Pi^{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}}{\Pi^{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}}\mathchoice{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\right)=\frac{1}{2}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}^{\text{I}}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\alpha\beta}.

(61)

Taking derivatives with respect the field
\bar{\mathfrak{h}}
, we obtain the two-point function:

{\Pi}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}^{\text{I}}(k)={\Pi}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}(k)+\frac{\delta\mathcal{G}^{\pi\tau}_{\lambda}}{\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}}{\Pi}_{\pi\tau}{}^{\lambda}{}_{\alpha\beta}(k)+{\Pi}^{\mu\nu\rho\sigma}{}_{\gamma}(k)\frac{\delta\mathcal{G}_{\rho\sigma}^{\gamma}}{\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\alpha\beta}}+\frac{\delta\mathcal{G}^{\pi\tau}_{\lambda}}{\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}}{\Pi}_{\pi\tau}{}^{\lambda}{}_{\rho\sigma}{}^{\gamma}(k)\frac{\delta\mathcal{G}^{\rho\beta^{\prime}}_{\gamma}}{\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\alpha\beta}},

(62)

where the derivatives are taken with
\bar{\mathfrak{h}}=0
. From the equation of motion (
13
), we find that the proper self-energy can be written as

{\bm{\Pi}}^{\text{I}}={\bm{\Pi}}_{\bar{\mathfrak{h}}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}}+\kappa\bm{B}\bm{M}^{-1}(\eta){\bm{\Pi}}_{\bar{G}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}}-\kappa{\bm{\Pi}}_{\bar{\mathfrak{h}}\bar{G}}\bm{M}^{-1}(\eta)\bm{C}-\kappa^{2}\bm{B}\bm{M}^{-1}(\eta){\bm{\Pi}}_{\bar{G}\bar{G}}\bm{M}^{-1}(\eta)\bm{C},

(63)

where we used the matrices
{\bm{\Pi}}_{\bar{\mathfrak{h}}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}}
,

{\bm{\Pi}}_{\bar{G}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}}
,

{\bm{\Pi}}_{\bar{\mathfrak{h}}\bar{G}}
and
{\bm{\Pi}}_{\bar{G}\bar{G}}
that denote respectively the self-energies
\bar{\mathfrak{h}}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}
,
\bar{G}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}
,
\bar{\mathfrak{h}}\bar{G}
and
\bar{G}\bar{G}
and
\bm{B}
,
\bm{C}
and
\bm{M}(\eta)=\bm{D}
defined in Eq. (
42
).

Using Eq. (
42
), we obtain that

\begin{split}[\bm{B}\bm{M}^{-1}(\eta)]{}^{\pi\tau\ \mu\nu}{}_{\lambda}={}&-[\bm{M}^{-1}(\eta)\bm{C}]{}^{\mu\nu}{}_{\lambda}{}^{\pi\tau}\\
={}&\frac{i}{4}\left[k^{\pi}(\delta_{\lambda}^{\nu}\eta^{\mu\tau}+\delta_{\lambda}^{\mu}\eta^{\nu\tau})+k^{\tau}(\eta^{\pi\nu}\delta_{\lambda}^{\mu}+\eta^{\pi\mu}\delta_{\lambda}^{\nu})-k_{\lambda}\left(\eta^{\pi\nu}\eta^{\mu\tau}+\eta^{\pi\mu}\eta^{\nu\tau}+\frac{2\eta^{\pi\tau}\eta^{\mu\nu}}{2-{{D}}}\right)\right].\end{split}

(64)

Substituting this and the self-energies computed above in Eq. (
63
), we obtain the divergent part of the proper self-energy:

\begin{split}\Pi^{\text{I div}}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}={}&\frac{\kappa^{2}k^{4}}{16\pi^{2}\epsilon}\sum_{J=I}^{V}\sum_{m=1}^{5}\mathcal{C}_{m}^{(J)}(\xi)\mathcal{T}^{(m)}{}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}(k)=\frac{\kappa^{2}k^{4}}{16\pi^{2}\epsilon}\sum_{m=1}^{5}C_{m}(\xi)\mathcal{T}^{(m)}{}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}(k),\end{split}

(65)

where
the coefficients
\mathcal{C}_{m}^{(J)}
are presented in Table
4
. Summing all entries of the
m
-row yields the coefficient
{C}_{m}(\xi)
shown in Eq. (
24
).

). The total of each row yields

).

The Ward identity Eq. (
23
) satisfied by the proper self-energy (
65
) implies that the coefficients
C_{i}(\xi)
are not independent. Indeed, imposing it to a general tensor

(k^{\mu}\eta^{\rho\nu}+k^{\nu}\eta^{\rho\mu}-k^{\rho}\eta^{\mu\nu})\sum_{i=1}^{5}C_{i}\mathcal{T}^{(i)}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}(k)=0,

one finds that

C_{1}=4(C_{2}+C_{3}),\quad C_{4}=\frac{C_{1}}{2}\quad\text{and}\quad C_{5}=-C_{3},

(66)

which are the same conditions satisfied by
C_{i}(\xi)
in Eq. (
24
).

A.3.1
Counterterm Lagrangian

The counterterm Lagrangian can be obtained from the divergent part of the proper effective action (
61
). To connect these expressions, we note that the invariants constructed from

\displaystyle\bar{R}={}

\displaystyle\kappa k^{2}L_{\alpha\beta}\left(\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\alpha\beta}\eta_{\pi\tau}-I^{\alpha\beta}_{\pi\tau}\right)\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\pi\tau}+O(\kappa^{2}),

(67a)

\displaystyle\bar{R}_{\mu\nu}={}

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa}{2}\left[k_{\mu}k_{\nu}L_{\rho\sigma}-\frac{k^{2}}{2}\left(L_{\mu\sigma}L_{\rho\nu}+L_{\mu\rho}L_{\sigma\nu}\right)\right]\left(\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\rho\sigma}\eta_{\pi\tau}-I^{\rho\sigma}_{\pi\tau}\right)\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\pi\tau}+O(\kappa^{2});

(67b)

where
I_{\mu\nu}^{\alpha\beta}=(\delta_{\mu}^{\alpha}\delta_{\nu}^{\beta}+\delta_{\mu}^{\beta}\delta_{\nu}^{\alpha})/2
, are decomposed in the basis
\{\mathcal{T}^{(i)}\}
as

\displaystyle\sqrt{-\bar{g}}\bar{R}^{2}={}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\kappa^{2}k^{4}\left(4\mathcal{T}^{(1)}+\mathcal{T}^{(2)}+2\mathcal{T}^{(4)}\right)_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\alpha\beta},

(68a)

\displaystyle\sqrt{-\bar{g}}\bar{R}_{\mu\nu}\bar{R}^{\mu\nu}={}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\kappa^{2}k^{4}\left(4\mathcal{T}^{(1)}+\mathcal{T}^{(3)}+2\mathcal{T}^{(4)}-\mathcal{T}^{(5)}\right)_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\alpha\beta}.

(68b)

The proper effective action in Eq. (
61
) can thus be written as

\frac{1}{2}\frac{\kappa^{2}k^{4}}{16\pi\epsilon}\left\{C_{2}(\xi)\left[4\mathcal{T}^{(1)}+\mathcal{T}^{(2)}+2\mathcal{T}^{(4)}\right]_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}(k)+C_{3}(\xi)\left[4\mathcal{T}^{(1)}+\mathcal{T}^{(3)}+2\mathcal{T}^{(4)}-\mathcal{T}^{(5)}\right]_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}(k)\right\}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\alpha\beta}

(69)

and by using Eq. (
68
), we obtain that

\left.\mathcal{L}_{\text{CT}}^{\text{I}}\right|_{\bar{G}=\mathcal{G}}=\frac{\sqrt{-\bar{g}}}{16\pi^{2}\epsilon}\left[2C_{2}(\xi)\bar{R}^{2}+4C_{3}(\xi)\bar{R}_{\mu\nu}\bar{R}^{\mu\nu}\right],

(70)

which is equal to the result shown in Eq. (
26
).

Appendix B
Counterterms in the Yang-Mills theory

The analogous of the Eq. (
14
) in the Yang-Mills theory is given by:

\int\mathop{\mathcal{D}\mathfrak{F}^{a\,\mu\nu}}\exp i\int\mathop{dx}\left(\mathcal{L}^{\text{I}}_{\text{YM}}-\mathcal{L}^{\text{II}}_{{\text{YM}}}\right)=\exp\frac{i}{2}\int\mathop{d^{4}x}\left[\bar{F}_{\mu\nu}^{a}-f_{\mu\nu}^{a}(\bar{A})\right]\left[\frac{1}{2}\left(\bar{F}^{a\,\mu\nu}-f^{a\,\mu\nu}(\bar{A})\right)-gf^{abc}\mathfrak{A}^{b\,\mu}\mathfrak{A}^{c\,\nu}\right],

(71)

where
\bar{A}
,
\bar{F}
are respectively the background gauge and independent fields,
\mathfrak{A}
,
\mathfrak{F}
are the corresponding quantum fields and
f_{\mu\nu}^{a}(\bar{A})\equiv\partial_{\mu}\bar{A}^{a}_{\nu}-\partial_{\nu}\bar{A}^{a}_{\mu}+gf^{abc}\bar{A}^{b}_{\mu}\bar{A}^{c}_{\nu}
(for further details on the first order formulation of the Yang-Mills in the background field method, see Ref.
Brandt et al. 2019
).

Eq. (
71
) shows that when the background field
\bar{F}_{\mu\nu}^{a}
is on-shell:
\bar{F}_{\mu\nu}^{a}=f_{\mu\nu}^{a}(\bar{A})
, the first and the second order Yang-Mills Lagrangians, in the background field method, turn out to be equivalent. Following the same reasoning in Sec. II, one can show that this also holds for the effective actions when the background field
\bar{F}^{a}_{\mu\nu}
is on-shell (and the source of the complementary field set to zero).

Now, we consider the contributions to the gluon self-energy in the first order formulation
Brandt et al. 2019
illustrated in Fig.
2
.

Figure 2:
Divergent part of the background self-energies in the first-order Yang-Mills theory. The factor
d_{N}
is equal to
iNg^{2}\delta^{ab}/16\pi^{2}\epsilon
.

The divergent parts of these contributions yield the first order counterterm Lagrangian

\mathcal{L}^{\text{I}}_{\text{CT}}=-\frac{1}{2}d_{N}\left[C_{\bar{F}\bar{F}}\bar{F}_{\mu\nu}^{a}\bar{F}^{a\,\mu\nu}+2C_{\bar{F}\bar{f}}\bar{F}_{\mu\nu}^{a}f^{a\,\mu\nu}(\bar{A})+C_{\bar{f}\bar{f}}f_{\mu\nu}^{a}(\bar{A})f^{a\,\mu\nu}(\bar{A})\right],

(72)

where

d_{N}=g^{2}N/16\pi^{2}\epsilon
and

C_{\bar{F}\bar{F}}=\frac{\xi+1}{2},\quad C_{\bar{F}\bar{f}}=1,\quad C_{\bar{f}\bar{f}}=\frac{7-3\xi}{6}.

(73)

Using the on-shell condition
\bar{F}_{\mu\nu}^{a}=f_{\mu\nu}^{a}(\bar{A})
, the expression (
72
) reduces to

\left.\mathcal{L}^{\text{I}}_{\text{CT}}\right|_{\bar{F}=f(\bar{A})}=-\frac{1}{2}d_{N}\frac{11}{3}f_{\mu\nu}^{a}(\bar{A})f^{a\,\mu\nu}(\bar{A})

(74)

which agrees with the counterterm Lagrangian obtained in the second order formalism (see Ref.
Abbott 1982
).

One can also verify that the 2-point Green’s function with external
\bar{A}
fields, are equivalent. In terms of the self-energies shown in Fig.
2
, such an equivalence of the propagators may be written in a compact form, as

D_{\bar{A}\bar{F}}\Pi^{\text{I}}_{\bar{F}\bar{F}}D_{\bar{F}\bar{A}}+2D_{\bar{A}\bar{F}}\Pi^{\text{I}}_{\bar{F}\bar{A}}D_{\bar{A}\bar{A}}+D_{\bar{A}\bar{A}}\Pi^{\text{I}}_{\bar{A}\bar{A}}D_{\bar{A}\bar{A}}=D_{\bar{A}\bar{A}}\Pi^{\text{II}}_{\bar{A}\bar{A}}D_{\bar{A}\bar{A}},

(75)

where the tree propagators
D_{\bar{x}\bar{y}}
are the same obtained for the quantum fields, the self-energies in first order formalism are given in Fig.
2
and
\Pi^{\text{II}}_{\bar{A}\bar{A}}
is the gluon self-energy in second order formalism (see Eqs. 3.3.1–3 of Ref.
Abbott 1982
).
A straightforward calculation shows that the above propagators are transverse and that (
75
) leads in momentum space to the equation

\left(C_{\bar{F}\bar{F}}+2C_{\bar{F}\bar{f}}+C_{\bar{f}\bar{f}}\right)\left(k_{\mu}k_{\nu}-\eta_{\mu\nu}k^{2}\right)=\frac{11}{3}(k_{\mu}k_{\nu}-\eta_{\mu\nu}k^{2}),

(76)

which is satisfied due to the relations (
73
).

Appendix C
Background Ward identities

The background effective action
\bar{\Gamma}(\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu},\mathchoice{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}})
defined as the effective action in Eq. (
17a
) evaluated for vanishing mean quantum fields is invariant under the background gauge transformations (
11
)
Lavrov and Shapiro 2019
.
This implies that

\Delta\bar{\Gamma}(\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu},\mathchoice{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}})=\int\mathop{d^{4}x}\left(\Delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}\frac{\delta\bar{\Gamma}}{\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}}+\Delta\mathchoice{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\frac{\delta\bar{\Gamma}}{\delta\mathchoice{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}}\right)=0.

(77)

This relation leads to Ward identities that reflect the background gauge symmetry of this action.
Such identities are obtained by taking functional derivatives of Eq. (
77
) with respect to the background fields
\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\alpha\beta}
and
\mathchoice{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
, evaluated at vanishing background fields.

Replacing Eq. (
11
) in Eq. (
77
), we have

\begin{split}&\left[\frac{1}{\kappa}(\eta^{\lambda\nu}\partial_{\lambda}\zeta^{\mu}+\eta^{\mu\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}\zeta^{\nu}-\eta^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\lambda}\zeta^{\lambda})+\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\lambda\nu}\partial_{\lambda}\zeta^{\mu}+\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}\zeta^{\nu}-\partial_{\lambda}(\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}\zeta^{\lambda})\right]\frac{\delta\bar{\Gamma}}{\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}}\\
&+\left[-\partial_{\mu}\partial_{\nu}\zeta^{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2}(\delta_{\mu}^{\lambda}\partial_{\nu}+\delta_{\nu}^{\lambda}\partial_{\mu})\partial_{\rho}\zeta^{\rho}-\zeta^{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\mathchoice{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}+\mathchoice{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\partial_{\rho}\zeta^{\lambda}-(\mathchoice{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.0113pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.0113pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.48586pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.91846pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.0113pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.0113pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.48586pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.91846pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textsty scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}}=0.\end{split}

(78)

For instance, taking a derivative with respect to the background metric
\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\alpha\beta}
, it is straightforward to derive the following relation between the self-energies
\bar{\mathfrak{h}}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}
and
\bar{G}\bar{\mathfrak{h}}
:

i(k^{\mu}\eta^{\nu\rho}+k^{\nu}\eta^{\mu\rho}-k^{\rho}\eta^{\mu\nu}){\Pi}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}(k)-\kappa\left[k^{\mu}k^{\nu}\delta_{\lambda}^{\rho}-\frac{k^{\rho}}{2}\left(k^{\mu}\delta_{\lambda}^{\nu}+k^{\nu}\delta_{\lambda}^{\mu}\right)\right]{\Pi}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\lambda}{}_{\alpha\beta}(k)=0.

(79)

Now, taking the derivative with respect to the field
\mathchoice{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
, one obtains

i(k^{\mu}\eta^{\nu\rho}+k^{\nu}\eta^{\mu\rho}-k^{\rho}\eta^{\mu\nu}){\Pi}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}{}^{\gamma}(k)-\kappa\left[k^{\mu}k^{\nu}\delta_{\lambda}^{\rho}-\frac{k^{\rho}}{2}\left(k^{\mu}\delta_{\lambda}^{\nu}+k^{\nu}\delta_{\lambda}^{\mu}\right)\right]{\Pi}_{\mu\nu}{}^{\lambda}{}_{\alpha\beta}{}^{\gamma}(k)=0,

(80)

which relates the self-energies
\bar{\mathfrak{h}}\bar{G}
and
\bar{G}\bar{G}
.
To derive these identities, we have used the fact that single functional derivatives
\delta\bar{\Gamma}/\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}^{\mu\nu}}
and
\delta\bar{\Gamma}/\delta\mathchoice{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
(tadpoles) vanish in our theory. Both identities were explicitly verified at tree and one-loop level.

C.1
On-Shell Ward identities

Using the equation of motion (
13
), the Eq. (
77
) reduces to

\Delta\bar{\Gamma}\left(\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu},\mathcal{G}(\bar{h})\right)=\int\mathop{d^{4}x}\Delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}\frac{\delta\Gamma^{\text{I}}}{\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}}=0,

(81)

where
\Gamma^{\text{I}}\equiv\bar{\Gamma}\left(\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu},\mathcal{G}(\bar{\mathfrak{h}})\right)
is the proper effective action. Taking a derivative of the above equation with respect to
\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\alpha\beta}
(and setting it to zero) yields the following expression

(\eta^{\mu\rho}k^{\nu}+\eta^{\nu\rho}k^{\mu}-\eta^{\mu\nu}k^{\rho})\Pi^{\text{I}}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}(k)=0.

(82)

This is the Ward identity satisfied by the proper self-energy (see Eq. (
23
)).

One also can show that Eq. (
82
) is a direct consequence of the Ward identities (
79
) and (
80
). First, let us consider the expression (
77
), when the independent field
\mathchoice{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
is on-shell:

\Delta\bar{\Gamma}\left(\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu},\bar{G}=\mathcal{G}\right)=\int\mathop{d^{4}x}\Delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}\left(\frac{\delta}{\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}}+\frac{\delta\mathchoice{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}}{\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}}\frac{\delta}{\delta\mathchoice{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}}\right)\bar{\Gamma}=0.

(83)

We define the operator

\mathsf{F}_{\mu\nu}\equiv\frac{\delta}{\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}}+\frac{\delta\mathchoice{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}}{\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}}\frac{\delta}{\delta\mathchoice{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}}

(84)

which encodes the relation between
\bar{\Gamma}
and
\Gamma^{\text{I}}
:

\mathsf{F}_{\mu\nu}\bar{\Gamma}=\frac{\delta}{\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}}\Gamma^{\text{I}}.

(85)

The Ward identities (
79
) and (
80
) correspond to the action of the operators

\frac{\delta}{\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\alpha\beta}}\mathsf{F}_{\mu\nu}\quad\text{and}\quad\frac{\delta}{\delta\mathchoice{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\gamma}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}}\mathsf{F}_{\mu\nu}

(86)

in the action
\bar{\Gamma}
.
This implies that

\int\mathop{d^{4}x}\,\Delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}\frac{\delta}{\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\alpha\beta}}\mathsf{F}_{\mu\nu}\bar{\Gamma}=0\quad\text{and}\quad\int\mathop{d^{4}x}\,\Delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}\frac{\delta\mathchoice{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\pi\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\pi\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\pi\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\pi\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}}{\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\alpha\beta}}\frac{\delta}{\delta\mathchoice{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\pi\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\pi\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\pi\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\pi\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}}\mathsf{F}_{\mu\nu}\bar{\Gamma}=0.

(87)

Adding them, we get

\int\mathop{d^{4}x}\,\Delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}\left(\frac{\delta}{\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\alpha\beta}}\mathsf{F}_{\mu\nu}+\frac{\delta\mathchoice{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\pi\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\pi\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\pi\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\pi\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}}{\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\alpha\beta}}\frac{\delta}{\delta\mathchoice{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\pi\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\pi\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\pi\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{G}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.36418pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.93517pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.52512pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}_{{{\pi\tau}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}}\mathsf{F}_{\mu\nu}\right)=\int\mathop{d^{4}x}\,\Delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}\mathsf{F}_{\alpha\beta}\mathsf{F}_{\mu\nu},

(88)

up to tadpole contributions. Using the relation (
85
) and Eq. (
83
), we derive that

\int\mathop{d^{4}x}\,\Delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}\mathsf{F}_{\alpha\beta}\mathsf{F}_{\mu\nu}\bar{\Gamma}=\int\mathop{d^{4}x}\,\Delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}\frac{\delta}{\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\alpha\beta}}\frac{\delta}{\delta\bar{\mathfrak{h}}^{\mu\nu}}\Gamma^{\text{I}}=0.

(89)

C.1.1
‘t Hooft identities

Let us consider the generating functional of Green’s functions in the second order EH theory

Z[j]=\int\mathop{\mathcal{D}h^{\mu\nu}}\exp i\int\mathop{d^{4}x}\left[\mathcal{L}_{\text{inv}}+j_{\mu\nu}h^{\mu\nu}\right],

(90)

where
\mathcal{L}_{\text{inv}}
is the gauge invariant EH Lagrangian and
j_{\mu\nu}
is an external source.
In order to extract an infinite gauge group factor, we introduce a functional
\Delta_{\text{FP}}[h]
defined as

\int\mathop{\mathcal{D}\zeta}\Delta_{\text{FP}}[h]\delta\left[\partial_{\mu}h^{\mu\nu}_{\zeta}-B^{\nu}\right]=\text{constant},

(91)

where
\Delta_{FP}[h]
yields the Faddeev-Popov ghost contributions.

Inserting Eq. (
91
) in Eq. (
90
), evaluated at
j_{\mu\nu}=0
, we get

Z[0]=\int\mathop{\mathcal{D}h^{\mu\nu}}\mathop{\mathcal{D}\zeta}\Delta_{\text{FP}}[h]\left[\partial_{\mu}h^{\mu\nu}_{\zeta}-B^{\nu}\right]\exp i\int\mathop{d^{4}x}\mathcal{L}_{\text{inv}}.

(92)

Next, making an inverse gauge transformation
h^{\mu\nu}\to h^{\mu\nu}_{\zeta^{-1}}
, we obtain

Z[0]=\int\mathop{\mathcal{D}h^{\mu\nu}}\Delta_{\text{FP}}[h]\left[\partial_{\mu}h^{\mu\nu}-B^{\nu}\right]\exp i\int\mathop{d^{4}x}\mathcal{L}_{\text{inv}},

(93)

where we used that
\Delta_{\text{FP}}[h]
is
\zeta
-independent and omitted an irrelevant infinite constant.
Because
Z[0]
is a gauge invariant quantity, it must be independent of
B^{\nu}
. Hence, integrating Eq. (
93
) over
B^{\nu}
with a gauge-breaking gaussian weight function

\rho=\exp\left[-\frac{1}{2\xi\kappa^{2}}\int\mathop{d^{4}x}(B^{\nu}-J^{\nu})^{2}\right],

(94)

where
\xi
is a gauge parameter and
J^{\nu}(x)
is an arbitrary function, we obtain the result

Z[0]=\int\mathop{\mathcal{D}h^{\mu\nu}}\Delta_{\text{FP}}[h]\exp i\int\mathop{d^{4}x}\left[\mathcal{L}_{\text{inv}}-\frac{1}{2\xi\kappa^{2}}\left(\partial_{\mu}h^{\mu\nu}-J^{\nu}\right)^{2}\right]

(95)

which is, in fact, independent of
J^{\nu}
.

Thus, expanding Eq. (
95
) in powers of
J^{\mu}(x)J^{\nu}(y)
, all coefficients of
J^{\mu}
must vanish. For instance, setting the coefficient of
J^{\mu}(x)J^{\nu}(y)
to zero, we obtain the identity

\frac{2}{\xi\kappa^{2}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x^{\rho}}\frac{\partial}{\partial y^{\sigma}}\langle 0|Th^{\rho\mu}(x)h^{\rho\nu}(y)|0\rangle=\eta^{\mu\nu}\delta^{4}(x-y).

(96)

This relation can be easily verified at tree level by using the free graviton propagator
\mathcal{D}_{\mu\rho\gamma\beta}
. Eq. (
96
) may be written at one-loop order, in the form

k_{\mu}k_{\nu}\mathcal{D}_{\mu\rho\gamma\beta}(k)\Pi^{\gamma\beta\gamma^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}(k)\mathcal{D}_{\gamma^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}\nu\rho}(k)=0,

(97)

where
\Pi^{\gamma\beta\gamma^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}(k)
is the graviton self-energy. This equation implies the relation

(\eta_{\mu\rho}k_{\nu}+\eta_{\nu\rho}k_{\mu}-\eta_{\mu\nu}k_{\rho})\Pi^{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}(k)(\eta_{\alpha\sigma}k_{\beta}+\eta_{\beta\sigma}k_{\alpha}-\eta_{\alpha\beta}k_{\sigma})=0.

(98)

We also have verified that the proper self-energy computed in Appendix
A
satisfies the ’t Hooft identity (
98
).

Appendix D
Counterterms in the second order formulation

In Ref.
Gonçalves et al. 2018
, the counterterms of the EH theory in second order form are computed for a general reparametrization of the metric field

g_{\alpha\beta}^{\prime}=\left[\bar{g}_{\alpha\beta}+\kappa(\gamma_{1}\phi_{\alpha\beta}+\gamma_{2}\phi\,\bar{g}_{\alpha\beta})+\kappa^{2}\left(\gamma_{3}\phi_{\alpha\rho}\phi^{\rho}{}_{\beta}+\gamma_{4}\phi_{\rho\omega}\phi^{\rho\omega}\bar{g}_{\alpha\beta}+\gamma_{5}\phi\,\phi_{\alpha\beta}+\gamma_{6}\phi^{2}\bar{g}_{\alpha\beta}\right)\right].

(99)

They find that the divergent part of the counterterms is given by (we set
\Lambda=0
, and we recall that we work in
{{D}}=4-2\epsilon
)

\Gamma^{(1)}_{\text{div}}=-\mu^{{{D}}-4}\frac{1}{16\pi^{2}\epsilon}\int d^{4}x\,\sqrt{-\bar{g}}\left\{g_{1}\bar{R}_{\mu\nu}^{2}+\left(\frac{g_{4}}{2}-\frac{1}{3}g_{1}\right)\bar{R}^{2}+\frac{g_{1}+g_{2}}{2}E_{4}+\frac{g_{3}}{2}\Box\bar{R},\right\}

(100)

where
\mu
is an arbitrary scale factor,
\phi=\phi_{\alpha}^{\alpha}
,
\Box=\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\mu}\bar{\mathsf{D}}^{\mu}
and

E_{4}=\bar{R}_{\mu\nu\rho\sigma}\bar{R}^{\mu\nu\rho\sigma}-4\bar{R}_{\mu\nu}\bar{R}^{\mu\nu}+\bar{R}^{2}

(101)

is the Gauss-Bonnet term. In
{{D}}=4
,
E_{4}
vanishes identically. The last term is a total derivative, and it also vanishes.

Thus, the counterterm Lagrangian for the general metric field in Eq. (
99
) reads

\mathcal{L}_{\text{CT}}^{\text{II}}=\frac{\sqrt{-\bar{g}}}{16\pi^{2}\epsilon}\left[g_{1}\bar{R}_{\mu\nu}^{2}+\left(\frac{g_{4}}{2}-\frac{g_{1}}{3}\right)\bar{R}^{2}\right],

(102)

where

g_{1}={}\frac{7}{20}+\frac{4\gamma_{3}^{2}}{\gamma_{1}^{4}}-\frac{2A^{2}}{\gamma_{1}^{2}B^{2}},\quad g_{4}={}\frac{1}{4}-\frac{9E}{2}+\frac{31\gamma_{3}^{2}+216\gamma_{4}(\gamma_{3}+2\gamma_{4})}{6\gamma_{1}^{4}}-\frac{A^{2}}{3\gamma_{1}^{2}B^{2}}-\frac{C}{6B^{2}}+\frac{C^{2}}{2B^{4}},\\

(103)

and

A={}\gamma_{3}+2\gamma_{5},\quad B={}\gamma_{1}+4\gamma_{2},\quad C={}(\gamma_{3}+4\gamma_{4})+4(\gamma_{5}+4\gamma_{6}),\quad E={}\frac{\gamma_{3}+4\gamma_{4}}{\gamma_{1}^{2}}.

(104)

D.1
’t Hooft and Veltman metric

Using the usual metric field definition, that is,

\gamma_{1}={}1,\quad\gamma_{2}=\gamma_{3}=\gamma_{4}=\gamma_{5}=\gamma_{6}=0;

(105)

one obtains the well-known result of ’t Hooft and Veltman
’t Hooft and Veltman 1974
:

\frac{\sqrt{-\bar{g}}}{16\pi^{2}\epsilon}\left[\frac{1}{120}\bar{R}^{2}+\frac{7}{20}\bar{R}_{\mu\nu}\bar{R}^{\mu\nu}\right].

(106)

D.2
Goldberg metric

In this work, we use the Goldberg metric field
h^{\mu\nu}=\sqrt{-g}g^{\mu\nu}
. Under the expansion of the metric as
g^{\mu\nu}\to\bar{g}^{\mu\nu}+\kappa\phi^{\mu\nu}
, we get

h_{\alpha\beta}^{\prime}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{-g}}g_{\alpha\beta}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{-\bar{g}}}\left(1+\frac{\kappa}{2}\phi+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{8}\phi^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\phi_{\mu\nu}\phi^{\mu\nu}+\cdots\right)\left(\bar{g}_{\alpha\beta}-\kappa\phi_{\alpha\beta}+\kappa^{2}\phi_{\alpha\mu}\phi^{\mu}_{\beta}+\cdots\right).

(107)

Comparing this with Eq. (
99
), we identify:

\gamma_{1}={}-1,\quad\gamma_{2}={}\frac{1}{2},\quad\gamma_{3}={}1,\quad\gamma_{4}={}-\frac{1}{4},\quad\gamma_{5}={}-\frac{1}{2}\quad\text{and}\quad\gamma_{6}={}\frac{1}{8}.

(108)

Using these parameters, we obtain the counterterm Lagrangian for the Goldberg metric in the second order formulation

\mathcal{L}_{\text{CT}}^{\text{II}}=\frac{\sqrt{-\bar{g}}}{16\pi^{2}\epsilon}\left[-\frac{119}{120}\bar{R}^{2}+\frac{87}{20}\bar{R}_{\mu\nu}\bar{R}^{\mu\nu}\right].

(109)

This result is valid only for
\xi=1
, corresponding to the De Donder–Feynman gauge. This is a special case of the general result in Eq. (
26
).

Then, we see that the counterterms are both gauge and parametrization dependent. However, the on-shell counterterm Lagrangian must be independent of both, as discussed in Ref.
Gonçalves et al. 2018
. Indeed, when Eq. (
102
) is evaluated on-shell, we find that

\mathcal{L}^{\text{II}}_{\text{CT}}|_{\text{on-shell}}=0.

(110)
